金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使 Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元；公司同時將 2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%，反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險，油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變，但短線波動勢將加劇：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期，可見 AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加、期限溢酬及利率波動影響，因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

Document 100: 2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し ～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～

Economic Trends

2026年11月 17日

1/3 
2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し 
～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 2027年の春闘賃上げ率を、厚生労働省ベースで 5.15％（2026年：5.18％）、連合ベースで
4.98％（同 5.01％）と予測する。2027年春闘でもほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げが続き、厚生労
働省ベースでは４年連続で５％台が実現するだろう。連合は 2027 年春闘の要求目安を前年
よりやや引き上げ、経団連も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。ＵＡゼンセ
ンも前年並みの高い要求水準を示すなど、労使双方の賃上げ姿勢にトーンダウンはみられな
い。 
○ 構造的な人手不足が続くなか、人材の確保・定着に向けた賃上げの必要性は高い。企業収益
も総じて堅調であり、高水準の利益が賃上げ余力を支えている。2026年の物価上昇率が前年
から鈍化することは賃上げ率の下押し要因となる。一方、春闘交渉が本格化する 2027年初に
は物価上昇率が再び高まる可能性があり、実質賃金の安定的な改善を求める労使双方の問題
意識も強い。賃上げ率が一段と加速することまでは見込みにくいものの、高い賃上げを継続
する環境は維持されている。 
○ ４年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現すれば、日本銀行にとっても金融政策の正常化に向けて前
向きな材料となる。特に、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを前提として賃
金を設定するようになっているかどうかが、２％の物価安定目標の定着を見極めるうえで重
要となる。

2027年春闘でも前年並みの高い賃上げ率を予想 
2027年の春闘賃上げ率を 5.15％と予測する（厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結
状況」ベース）。2024年春闘では5.33％、2025年では 5.52％、2026年でも 5.18％と３年連続で
５％台の高い賃上げが実現したが、2027年もほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げが続き、４年連続で５％
台となるだろう。 
春闘においては、厚生労働省よりも公表時期が早い、連合による集計値が注目されることが多
い。この連合ベースの数字では、2027年の春闘賃上げ率を 4.98％と予測する（2026年春闘：
5.01％）。５％台に乗るかどうかは微妙なところだが、こちらも歴史的にみれば高い賃上げとなる

### Page 2

2/3 
だろう。

組合側、経営側ともトーンダウンはみられず  
組合側、経営側とも賃上げに前向きな姿勢を維持している。連合は 10月22日に公表した2027
年春闘基本構想において、定昇込みの賃上げ要求を「６％中心、５％以上」、ベースアップ等の賃
上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とし、前年の賃上げ「５％以上」、ベア「３％以上」よりも要求
姿勢をやや強めた。経営側も賃上げの継続に前向きだ。経団連の経営労働政策委員会報告原案で
は、高い賃上げの「社会全体への浸透」が掲げられていると報じられた。また、筒井経団連会長
は 11月９日の記者会見で、ベア実施の検討を賃金交渉のスタンダードとする方針を堅持する考え
を示した。また、主要産業別労働組合組織では、ＵＡゼンセンが定昇込み６％、ベア等４％とい
う前年と同等の高い要求方針素案を示している。中東情勢悪化によるコスト上昇という逆風があ
るなかでも、労使ともに賃上げ姿勢がトーンダウンしている様子は窺えない。もっとも、連合の
要求目安は強まったものの、ＵＡゼンセンの要求率は前年と同水準であり、現時点では賃上げ率
のさらなる加速を見込むほどの材料が揃っているわけではないことには注意が必要だ。

物価は前年から鈍化も、人手不足と好調な企業収益が押し上げ要因に 
高い賃上げを支える最大の要因は、構造的な人手不足である。生産年齢人口の減少が続くな
か、企業の人手不足感は依然として強く、人材の確保・定着は重要な経営課題となっている。特
に若年層を中心に採用競争が厳しくなっており、既存人材のつなぎ止めのためにも企業は継続的
な賃上げを行う必要がある。ここ数年の春闘では、こうした人手不足を背景に高い賃上げが実現
しており、企業の賃金設定行動にも変化が生じている。2027年春闘でも人手不足を背景とした賃
上げ圧力は強いと考えられる。 
好調な企業収益も、高い賃上げを支える材料になる。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇は企業収
益の下押し要因となっているものの、価格転嫁の進展やＡＩ関連需要の拡大などを背景に、企業
収益は総じて堅調に推移しており、水準も非常に高い。企業規模や業種によって差はあるもの
の、企業収益が全体として高水準にあり、賃上げ余力は十分に確保されていると見てよい。 
一方、物価については、2026年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍化することが賃上げ率の下押し要因
になるとみられる。春闘交渉では前年の暦年の物価上昇率が参照されることが多いが、2026年の
上昇率（ＣＰＩ総合）は前年比＋２％前後と、2025年の前年比＋3.2％から鈍化する見込みであ
り、物価高への対応という観点からの賃上げ圧力は弱まる可能性がある。 
もっとも、暦年でみれば物価上昇率は鈍化する一方、春闘交渉が本格化する 2027年初には再び
高まり、ＣＰＩは前年比＋３％台に達する可能性がある。この場合、実質賃金が再びマイナスに
転じる恐れもある。そもそも、過去数年にわたって実質賃金が減少してきたことで、家計には賃
上げの恩恵を十分に実感できないという不満が残っている。実質賃金の改善の遅れへの問題意識
は組合側、経営側にも共有されており、実質賃金を安定的に増加させることは 2027年春闘でも重
要な課題となるだろう。春闘交渉の時点で物価上昇率の再加速が意識されれば、春闘賃上げ率の

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
上振れ要因となる可能性もあることには注意したい。

２％「定着」判断の重要な材料に  
以上を踏まえ、2027年春闘では5.15％と、ほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げを予想する（厚生労働省
ベース）。賃上げ率が一段と加速することまでは見込みにくいものの、高い賃上げを継続する環境
は維持されていると考えられる。 
４年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現すれば、賃金上昇の持続性を重視する日本銀行にとっても前
向きな材料となるだろう。特に重要なのは、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを
前提として賃金を設定するようになっているかどうかである。2026年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍
化するなかでも高い賃上げが維持されれば、こうした賃金設定が広がりつつあることを示す一つ
の材料となる。日銀は基調的な物価上昇率が２％の物価安定目標とおおむね整合的な水準に達し
たとの認識を示したが、２％の物価安定目標が「定着」したとまでは判断していない。2027年春
闘は、その定着を見極めるうえでも重要な判断材料となるだろう。

Giant Manufacturing

3Q26 EPS of NT$0.85 missed on margins

Maintain Rating: UNDERPERFORM | PO: 87.00 TWD | Price: 88.00 TWD

3Q26 EPS of NT$0.85 missed on margins  
Giant reported 3Q26 EPS of NT$0.85 (-4% YoY), which missed our estimate of NT$1.16 
on margins. The miss was due to higher than expected SG&A expenses as we believe the 
company continues to invest in marketing to support its new product launches as well as 
legal fees related to the WRO issues. 3Q26 revenue was +6% YoY to NT$16.3bn, which 
was in-line with preliminary revenue. 3Q26 gross margin was +2.4pps YoY to 23.9%. 
OPM was +0.5pps YoY to 3.1%.  
Oct revenue was up 2% YoY  
Oct revenue was up by 2% YoY. Growth in OB revenue was offset by lower OE 
contribution. Within OB business, Europe turned to a single digit decline while China 
achieved double digit growth. US reported steep decline due to weak market demand 
and negative impact from WRO investigation. We expect revenue growth to remain 
unexciting in 4Q26 as there should be limited product launches to drive sales during the 
winter season.  
Cut PO to N$87; Maintain Underperform  
We cut 2027E EPS by 7% to reflect 3Q26 miss. Based on unchanged 18x 2027E P/E, we 
cut our PO by 7% to NT$87. We expect end demand to remain under pressure due to 
macro uncertainty and inflation. We see downside risks to VA consensus EPS growth of 
83% YoY to NT$6.1 for 2027E given uncertainty in sales recovery and margins 
normalization.

Company Sector 
Leisure

Company Description 
Established in 1972, Giant is the largest global bicycle 
manufacturer. Giant operates its own brand business 
(OBM) (68% of revenue) under Giant (mid-to-high-end 
bikes), Liv (women's), and Momentum (low-end) brand 
names. Giant's largest OEM customers are Trek (16% of 
revenue) and Scott (13% of revenue). Giant generated 39% 
of revenue from Europe, 23% from North America, 12% 
from China and 26% from Others in 2020. Its production is 
mainly based in China and Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We rate Giant shares Underperform. We see high 
uncertainty in earnings recovery given weak end demand 
and negative earnings impact from FX/tariff headwinds.

Price to Book Value 1.0x

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Giant Manufacturing (GTMUF) 
Our NT$87 price objective is based on 18x 2027E P/E. The target multiple of 18x is at 
0.5SD below the long-term average of 25x (2011-2025) given higher macro uncertainty 
and slow earnings growth.

Downside risks to our price objective are COVID resurgence to further disrupt supply 
chain and unexpectedly cool-down in bike demand. 
Upside risks to our price objective are stronger-than-expected demand, fast capacity 
expansion and efficiency ramp-up, more favorable input costs and product mix shift.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

### Page 1

Better Interest Spread — Group interest spread widened 8bps qoq to 6.95%, 
mainly driven by better China spread. China spread improved 19bps qoq, as loan 
yield increased 8bps qoq and funding cost was 11bps lower qoq. Taiwan loan spread 
narrowed 7bps qoq, primarily due to 6bps qoq higher funding cost amid Fed rate 
hike and tightening domestic liquidity. 
Muted Loan Growth — Group loans grew by 1% qoq/4% yoy, mainly driven by 
ASEAN portfolio growth and RMB appreciation. Taiwan portfolio was flat qoq/-1% 
yoy. China portfolio was +2% qoq/flat yoy in local currency (+3% qoq/+11% yoy in 
NTD). Management suggested that the China business has achieved positive loan 
growth in the past three months and they expected positive momentum ahead. 
ASEAN portfolio was+2% qoq/+10% yoy,  
Broadly Stable China Asset Quality, Higher ASEAN Credit Cost — Group 
delinquency ratio slightly increased 3bps qoq to 5.16%. Taiwan delinquency ratio 
increased 20bps qoq, while NPL formation improved qoq. China delinquency ratio 
was stable qoq, but NPL formation slightly picked up qoq per our estimates. Group 
credit cost was 2.49%, +11bps qoq, mainly due to higher ASEAN credit costs on the 
back of macro uncertainties especially in Cambodia. 
Implications — Despite steady profit growth in 10M26 and stabilizing China asset 
quality, Chailease shares continued to de-rate this year amid concerns over growth. 
Management sounded more upbeat on China loan growth outlook at the results 
briefing, and the impact from auto financing business on Taiwan loan growth should 
gradually fade. FY26E dividend yield of 6% is attractive, in our view.

Chailease Holding (5871.TW)

2
Chailease Holding
Valuation
Our target price for Chailease of NT$130 is based on 1.2x 2026E P/B, using a Gordon Growth model. Our valuation
methodology takes into account a sustainable ROE estimate of 12.1%, a cost-of-equity assumption of 11.1%, and a long-term
growth rate of 6.0%. We use a blended approach to calculate both risk-free rates and long-term growth rates taking into
account local estimates on these parameters in the major markets in which Chailease operates, namely Taiwan, China, and

Thailand, and weighted them by asset mix.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Chailease stock from reaching our target price are: [1] slower-than-expected asset
growth momentum; [2] asset quality deterioration in different markets; [3] higher operating expenses, which drag bottom-line
growth; [4] earlier-than-expected capital-raising that dilutes ROE and BVPS; and [5] ASEAN expansion that is slow in
contributing to group earnings. Key upsides risks to our target price include: [1] faster-than-expected asset quality recovery as

macro uncertainties ease; [2] higher-than-expected asset growth momentum.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned
to a Catalyst Watch Upside, Catalyst Watch Downside, STV Upside, or STV Downside call is considered Catalyst Watch/STV

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q26 earnings beat on margins: Better GM of 32.5% (+1.8ppt QoQ) and well-
controlled opex of NT$3,349mn (opex ratio 12.0%) led to an operating profit of NT
$5,720mn (OPM 20.5%), 6%/11% higher than MSe/cons. at NT$5,390mn (OPM
18.9%)/NT$5,142mn (OPM 18.7%).
Power interconnects playing an increasingly important role: We estimate that
power was 65-70% of its HPC revenue in 3Q26 vs. ~50% in the past two years,
which should bring in tailwinds for its blended GM, as power interconnects in

general carry higher margins than data ones. Looking forward, we expect the
increasing power consumption in AI clusters to continue drive the growth from both
volume and spec upgrades in this space. Moreover, future power delivery
architecture could also add incremental opportunities for Bizlink.
Data interconnects diversify into optical: While the market has been concerned
about its data interconnect business recently owing to the focus on the copper

camp, we’d point out that the revenue mix from data interconnects decreased to 30-
35% of HPC in 3Q26 (or 15% of total revenue), per our estimates. Looking ahead, it
is diversifying into the optical space, including micro VCSEL based ALCs along with
its US partner, shuffle boxes/FAU alignment process for CPO and fiber patch cords.
Interplex contribution bigger than expected: Bizlink started to consolidate
Interplex from late Sep and it contributed US$67mn of revenue in Oct, or 18% of

Bizlink’s US$369mn (NT$11,846mn, +27% QoQ/+80% YoY). This is higher than the
market expectation of high single digit %. Our checks indicate that its margins are
above Bizlink’s corporate average, so the earnings might be over 20%, we estimate.
We believe this could make its offerings more comprehensive in the AI cluster and
Interplex could expand its business faster with the financial strength of Bizlink.
Stay OW: We think the growth outlook looks solid and is further strengthened by

the consolidation of Interplex. With the valuation of 11x 2027 P/E approaching the
trough valuation of 8x P/E post the recent pullback, we think risk reward looks
attractive. Stay OW with lower PT of NT$2,500 (18x 2027 P/E).

Results Summary
3Q26 earnings beat on margins
Off revenue of NT$27,875mn (+20% QoQ/+51% YoY), GM expanded by 1.8ppt to 32.5%,
vs. MSe/consensus at 32.0%/31.8%, on a more favorable revenue mix. Opex was also well
controlled at NT$3,349mn (opex ratio 12.0%) vs. MSe/consensus at NT$3,728mn (opex
ratio 13.1%)/NT$3,615mn (opex taio 13.1%), leading to an operating profit of NT$5,720mn
(OPM 20.5%), 6%/11% higher than MSe/consensus at NT$5,390mn (OPM 18.9%)/NT

$5,142mn (OPM 18.7%). With the non-op loss of NT$210mn, net profit came in at NT
$4,293mn (EPS NT$22.01), 7%/13% higher than MSe/consensus at NT$3,996mn (EPS NT
$20.67)/NT$3,789mn (EPS NT$19.27).

6
Valuation Methodology
Our new price target (base case scenario value) of  NT$2,500 (NT$3,665 previously) is
derived from 18x 2027e P/E (30x previously): We use price/earnings as our valuation
methodology because we believe it appropriately captures Bizlink's earnings growth
potential. We believe our target P/E multiple of 18x is justified by an earnings CAGR of
55%   over 2025-28, which implies a PEG of  0.3, lower than the average of 0.7 in 2019-24,

when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 13x for a 19% earnings CAGR. We use a
lower target multiple to factor in more conservative sentiment towards AI stocks.
Bull case scenario value – NT$4,100 (vs. prior 4,800): We apply a 30x P/E multiple (vs.
prior 40x) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes stronger AEC/ALC
demand owing to broader adoption within AI servers along with spec upgrades.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,100 (vs. prior 1,760): We apply a 8x P/E multiple (vs.

prior 15x) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes lukewarm data center
demand amid softening IT capex budgets and pricing pressure for low-barrier products in
other business segments.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

高毛利產品合計來到 59%，此外營運槓桿帶來的效益使本業獲利
率創歷史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700
萬美金，除了擴大產品線外，合併後同樣有助於加深既有客戶的合
作關係。展望 2027 年，HPC 事業將迎來產品價量齊揚，尤其以高
毛利的 Power 產品最為明顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步
起量，因此可預期 HPC 占比將會再進一步提升，利於整體營運表
現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2026 年 EPS 76.23 元，2027 年
EPS 131.41 元，目前 PE 為 11.6x，參考歷史 PE 區間為 10-35x，
考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔憂資料中心建置速
度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部屬之風險，
因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
貿聯-KY(3665)成立於 1996 年，為全球連接線大廠，總部位於美
國矽谷，生產據點則廣泛分布在全球 37 地。產品應用包含數據通
訊、工廠自動化、半導體設備、醫療、車用等，2025 年工業佔 35%、
通訊 42%、車用 11%、電器 11%、其他 1%。銷售主要為美國 20%、
中國 20%、德國 16%、馬來西亞 7%、台灣 3%、其他 34%。

殖利率(%) 0.87 HPC 部門成長 Q+30%，Y+61%，SPE 也延續上半年以來的穩健
成長幅度，Q+11%，Y+70%，兩高利潤事業合計貢獻營收占比進
一步攀升至 59%，Q+3ppts，Y+8ppts，在營收成長帶來的營運槓
桿效益下也反映在營益率創公司歷史新高。

Interplex 正式併表，產品組合有望再度優化 
貿聯 10 月營收 118.5 億元，M+26.9%，Y+80.0%，自 9 月起完成
對 Interplex 的收購，10 月已完整貢獻營收，單月貢獻金額高達
6700 萬美金，約 20 億台幣，由於 Interplex 既有客戶與貿聯本身
有所重疊，然而產品項目卻有所不同，預計在透過集團整合能力下
將進一步推動公司在客戶群內滲透率的提升。此外貿聯本身新規格
的 Power 產品也持續提高出貨，研究部預期產品組合有望再度優
化，進一步推升獲利率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 26Q4 營
收 349.1 億元，Q+25.3%，Y+76.5%，毛利率 33.84%，稅後淨利
56.7 億元，Q+32.0%，Y+106.8%，EPS 28.39 元；2026 年營收
1069.3 億元，Y+50.1%，毛利率 31.83%，稅後淨利 152.1 億元，
Y+69.0%，EPS 76.23 元。

2027 年 HPC 產品維持價量齊揚，光通訊產品 2028 年有望發酵 
隨著 Vera Rubin 進入放量，Rack Busbar 升級至 5000A，Power 
Whip 規格從 60A 升級至 100A 且用量增加，1.6T AEC 也將逐步
開始貢獻營收，另外 Interplex 的加入所帶來的產品優化和客戶關
係的綁定，研究部認為貿聯 HPC 事業仍將迎來顯著的價量齊揚增
長。光傳輸領域上貿聯也持續耕耘，除了新富生 MPO 相關產品，
OCP 上與 ams Osram 的合作也意味著公司已著手布局下一代
Scale up 的通訊方案，研究部看好 wide-and-slow 架構在未來的導
入機會以及市場，預計 2028 年後的發酵有望帶來營運成長動能。
綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2027 年營收 1608.0 億元，Y+50.4%，

結論 
高毛利產品合計來到 59%，此外營運槓桿帶來的效益使本業獲利率創歷
史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700 萬美金，除了
擴大產品線外，合併後同樣有助於加深既有客戶的合作關係。展望 2027
年，HPC 事業將迎來產品價量齊揚，尤其以高毛利的 Power 產品最為明
顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步起量，因此可預期 HPC 占比將
會再進一步提升，利於整體營運表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2026
年 EPS 76.23 元，2027 年 EPS 131.41 元，目前 PE 為 11.6x，參考歷
史 PE 區間為 10-35x，考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔
憂資料中心建置速度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部
屬之風險，因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。

 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500 원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC 으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500 억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O 에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7 억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

Document 269: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 11

### Page 1

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Earnings Review Company Analysis | 이차전지/화학 | 2026. 11. 4  
일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사 Capa 우려

AMPC 4,169억원 포함 영업이익 컨센서스 대폭 상회 
동사는 8일 매출액 9조 6,434억원(YoY 59%, QoQ 27%), AMPC 포함한 영업이익 
7,560억원(OPM 7.8%, YoY 26%, QoQ 567%)로 시장 Consensus(공정공시한 8일 
기준 3,264억원)을 대폭 상회하는 어닝 서프라이즈 발표. 회사는 ESS 출하 급증과 고
전압 Mid-Ni EV 파우치 확대, 견조한 EV향 2170-46시리즈 공급을 물량 중심을 성
장하였고, 폴란드 가동률 개선, 수익성이 견조한 원통형 물량 증가, 북미 ESS 생산성 
개선에 따른 고정비 부담 완화 및 고객사의 일회성 물량 보상금 영향으로 설명. 그러나 
일회성 물량 보상금의 구체적 금액, 반복 발생 여부는 제시하지 않아 불확실성 남겨. 
7,560억원의 영업이익에도 불구하고 영업외손실 9,872억원 발생으로 당기순손실 기
록. 이에는 소형 IT파우치 자산손상 반영되어, 가동률 개선 및 감가상각비 감소를 위해 
Capa를 조정하는 배터리 회사들의 대응 전략이 이번 실적에서 다시 확인

‘30년 ESS 수요 전망들 대비 국내사 Capa 배 이상 높아 
동사는 북미 ESS Capa 확대를 통한 탈중국 수요에 적극 대응하겠다는 전략. 파우치 
라인 추가 전환 및 신규 각형 라인 확보를 통해 북미 ESS Capa를 ‘26년말 50+에서 
‘27년 70+로 제시. 최근 타사에서 ‘26년 미국 ESS 수요를 80~100GWh, 혹은 
130+GWh로, ‘30년 두배 이상, 혹은 240+GWh로 수요 전망에 대해 답변한 바 있음. 
동사 전체 Capa 300GWh(‘25년 연말), SK On 179GWh, SDI 140GWh)임을 감안하
면 ‘30년 약 2배 수요를 가정하면 셀 3사 Capa의 32%에 달하는 수요, 만일 3배 수
요 가정하면 48.5% 수준. 북미 외 시장 가성비 중시 기조 및 중국 LFP 장악(‘25년 
97% M/S)으로 진입장벽 높음을 감안하면 미국 수요 중요. 이를 상쇄하기 위해 EV향 
배터리의 안정적인 공급 지속 필요하며, 이를 위해 동사 원통형은 TESLA의 비미국 시
장 판매량이, 파우치는 시장 자체 축소 지속 여부에 유의할 필요 
상대적으로 가장 견조한 출하량, 그러나 고평가 영역  
셀3사중 EV향 배터리 출하량 및 ESS 탑재에서 현재 가장 견조한 판매량을 보이고 있
으나, AMPC와 일회성 보상금이 영업손익의 상당부분을 견인한다는 점에서 수익성 취
약하다는 단점. 또한 ROE가 (-) 영역 임에도 PBR이 3.x내외로 거래되고 있어 고평가 
지적에서 자유롭기 어려워. 당분간 모회사인 LG화학의 PRS 발행에 따른 단기 오버행 
이슈도 관건. PBR 2.3x(‘25년대비 하락하는 ROE 감안, 20% 할인)적용 목표주가 
290,000원 제시, HOLD 유지. 주식 투자 매력 낮은 상황으로 판단

EV향 3분기 data  
이차전지에서 EV향 비중은 여전히 가장 높다. 타입별로 보면 3분기 글로벌 EV향 이차전
지에서 각형의 M/S가 83%로 지속 증가하고 있으며, 동사가 생산하는 원통형과 파우치의 
경우 합산 M/S가 17%로 하락하였다. 현재 Wh 기준 동사 EV향 배터리에서 3분기 파우
치가 55%, 원통형이 45%로 파우치 비중이 10% 원통형을 상회한다.

나타난다.

그림 9 셀 3 사 분기별 EV 향 탑재량 글로벌 M/S 추이: M/S 감소중

자료: SNE 리서치, LS증권 리서치센터

밸류에이션, 수익성 대비 고평가 영역  
동사는 ‘24년 ROE -4.9%, ‘25년 -5.2%에 이어 ‘26년 -4.4%, ‘27년 -3.4%로 (-) 영역이 
지속되는 것으로 예상한다. ‘24년과 ‘25년 순이익은 (+)지만, 해외 합작회사가 많고 이들 이
익을 연결함에 따라 지배주주순이익은 적자를 기록한 것을 보인다. JV 규모가 축소되고 있
어 이러한 순이익과 지배주주 순이익상의 괴리는 좁혀지겠지만, ROE가 (-)영역 지속을 예
상함에 따라 현재 거래 PBR은 고평가 상황으로 볼 수 있다. 물론 이것은 향후 수익 성장
에 대한 시장 기대가 반영된 것으로 볼 수 있지만, 이차전지 6개사 평균 Consensus 대비 
실제 수익이 ‘23년부터 지속 하향되고 있다는 점, ESS대비 여전히 판매비중이 높은 EV에서 
출하량 정체가 일어나고 있다는 점, 미국 ESS에서 여전히 높은 중국 M/S를 감안하면 이러
한 기대 수준에 대해 다시 검토할 필요가 있다. 이에 더해, 모회사 기준에서는 필요한 과정
이지만 동사 주가 측면에서는 LG화학의 PRS 발행도 단기 오버행으로 당분간 주가에 부담
을 줄 수 있다.

그림 10 셀 3 사, 양∙음극재 3 사 추정치 변화율: ‘23 년부터 추정치 지속 하향 조정

자료: DataGuide, LS증권 리서치센터

미국 ESS가 모든 것을 해결할 수 있나? 
‘25년 글로벌 ESS 시장의 97%를 중국배터리사가 공급했다. LFP 각형이 표준화된 시장인 
것이다. 미국 시장만 중국에 대한 관세 허들, 정책 허들로 한국 배터리사에 기회로 작용한
다. 그러나 ‘26년부터 시작된 FEOC와 관세추가 부담에도 불구, 상반기 미국 ESS 시장의 
76%을 (기타사의 국적을 알 수 없기에 제외하더라도) 중국 셀사가 차지했다. 만일 한국 배
터리 유관회사들이 전망하듯 ‘30년 미국 ESS 시장이 160~200GWh 이상, 240+GWh, 혹
은 240~300GWh가 된다고 해도 3사 전체 Capa의 50%가 채 되지 못한다. EV 수요가 뒷
받침되지 않는다면 미국 ESS만으로는 가동률을 80~90% 이상 올리기 어렵다는 의미다. 특
히 동사는 원통형과 파우치를 생산하고 있으나, 원통형은 북미외 지역 TESLA의 판매에, 
파우치는 올해 출하량이 감소하는 것으로 나타나고 있다. 파우치 고객의 각형 채택 비중 
증가를 감안하면 동사 EV향 배터리 출하량 증가를 확신하기는 어렵다.   
긍정적 기대와 동시에 냉정한 점검이 필요한 시점으로 판단한다.

LS Securities Research 6 
표2 LG 에너지솔루션 3Q26 실적발표 주요 Q&A 
[Q&A]           
Q1-1. 4Q26 실적 전망과 2027 년 큰 방향은? 
  A. 4Q26 ESS 출하량은 북미 수요 대응으로 QoQ 약 40% 증가 예상 
   유럽 고전압 Mid-Ni 파우치 및 원통형 물량도 연말까지 견조한 출하 지속 전망 
   3Q26 가동을 시작한 북미 JV 효과가 더해져 전사 매출은 QoQ 약 10% 성장 가능할 것으로 예상 
   가동률 개선과 사이트 안정적 램프업은 지속 추진하나 3Q26 일회성 요인 소멸의 기저효과 존재 
   4Q26 혼다 JV ESS CAPA 추가 가동 준비에 따른 초기 고정비 부담으로 수익성 일부 둔화 불가피

2027 년 사업계획은 수립 중이며 전방 수요 변수로 구체적 가이던스 제시는 어려운 단계 
    ESS: 북미 확보 수주 기반으로 유의미한 물량 성장 지속 기대 
    소형: 전략 고객향 EV 원통형과 46 시리즈 신규 애플리케이션 판매로 안정적 성장 예상 
    EV 파우치: 중저가 제품 물량과 북미 EV 향 생산품이 더해져 성장 예상 
   전 사업부 매출 성장 기대하나 핵심 제품 경쟁력·기존 CAPA 효율화·가동률 극대화·원가 개선에 집중 
   2027 년 매출 성장률·영업이익률 등 구체적 수치는 제시 X

Q1-2. 북미 PFE 요건 충족을 위한 LFP 공급망 전략과 중국 관련 주력 양극재 공급사 대응은? 
  A. 미국 생산세액공제 확보를 위해 직접재료비 내 PFE 소재 비중 요건을 충족하는 공급망 구축 필요 
   회사 설명상 PFE 소재 허용 비중은 2026 년 40%에서 2027 년 35% 이하로 강화. Non-PFE 비중과 혼동 유의 
   소재별 소싱 전략을 구체화해 준비 중이며 요건 충족은 차질 없이 진행될 것으로 판단 
   재료비 비중이 큰 양극재는 최근 계약한 국내 업체 외 복수 Non-PFE 업체 조달 옵션 확보

음극재·분리막·전해액도 기존 Non-PFE 공급망에 더해 신규 업체 적극 발굴 
   기존 원가 중심 최적화에서 정책·지정학 리스크를 고려한 공급망 안정성 강화로 전략 확대 
   협력사의 원가·기술·물량 대응력을 종합 평가하고 장기적으로 현지 협력사 발굴·현지화 추진 
   질문한 중국 관련 주력 공급사의 개별 지위·조달 비중에 대한 직접적인 상세 답변 X

Q2-1. ESS 사업의 수익성 전망 및 개선 방안은? 
  A. EV→ESS 라인 전환으로 수요에 맞는 생산체계를 구축하고 북미 고객 적기 공급에 주력 
   다수 사이트 동시 램프업에 어려움이 있었고 고정비 절감 노력에도 수익성 개선 속도는 시장 기대보다 느렸음 
   2026 년 말 50GWh 목표 셀 라인 램프업은 마무리 국면. Link CAPA 도 3Q26 에 이어 4Q26 추가 보강 예정 
   CAPA 안정화와 점진적인 고정비 부담 완화 기대 
   확보 CAPA 효율화, 소재 소싱 다변화로 원재료비·물류비 절감에 집중 
   AI 데이터센터 및 SI 프로젝트 비중 확대를 통한 고객 포트폴리오 최적화 추진 
   셀·시스템 고용량화로 원가 경쟁력을 높여 구조적 수익성 개선 추진 
   구체적 ESS 영업이익률 목표나 개선 폭은 제시 X

Q2-2. 북미 ESS 추가 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 빠른 시장 성장 및 Non-PFE 에 우호적인 정책으로 북미 현지 생산 셀 수요 급증 
   현재 계획된 CAPA 기준으로 2028 년까지 물량은 대부분 확정. 그 이후 물량 중심으로 고객 수주 협의 진행 
   기존 전력망·유틸리티 고객, 이들을 통한 AI 데이터센터 BESS 수요, 하이퍼스케일러 BTM 직접 구매 수요 포함 
   예상 수요가 공급 가능 CAPA 를 초과해 전략적으로 중요한 프로젝트 중심으로 미래 잔여 CAPA 운영 계획 
   추가 고객명·신규 계약량 등은 제시하지 않음. 예상 수요와 확정 수주를 구분

Q3-1. 46 시리즈의 구체적인 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 기존 OEM 부터 EV 스타트업까지 이미 440GWh 이상 물량 확보 
   추가로 다수 글로벌 OEM 과 협의 중이며 가까운 시일 내 의미 있는 규모의 수주 성과 기대 
   제품 경쟁력·지역별 생산 역량으로 46 시리즈 선도 입지를 강화하고 고객 포트폴리오 확대 계획 
   440GWh 는 확보 물량이며 연간 판매량이나 생산 CAPA 가 아님. 추가 계약 규모·고객명은 미공개

### Page 7

Company Report / LG에너지솔루션 / 2026. 11.4 
LS Securities Research 7

Q3-2. Tabless 2170 의 경쟁력과 수주·신규 시장 대응 현황은? 
  A. 기존 제품 대비 낮은 발열·저항으로 안전성 및 고출력에 유리해 HEV·BBU·로봇 등 다양한 용도에 적합 
   오창에서 전동공구용 제품은 이미 양산 중  
   BBU 향 LFP 2170 은 2Q27 양산 목표로 준비 중 
   HEV 향은 2027 년 말 애리조나에서 양산 계획  
   LFP 2170 은 NCM 대비 열안전성 테스트에서 우수한 성능을 보여 고객의 긍정적 피드백 확보 
   고객 니즈·수요에 적극 대응해 신시장 경쟁력 확보 추진. 수주 규모 등 정량 정보는 제시하지 않음

Q4-1. 재무 건전성 개선 방안과 2027 년 자금 조달 계획은? 
  A. 선제 CAPA 투자 자금은 저리 정책자금 장기차입 및 원화·외화 회사채로 안정적으로 조달해 왔음 
   주요 투자는 대부분 집행됐고 CAPEX 는 2025 년을 기점으로 유의미하게 감소 중 
   향후 영업현금흐름 확대분을 차입금 상환에 우선 활용, 자산 유동화를 통한 추가 재원 확보도 병행 
   차입금·금융비용 부담을 축소해 재무지표 개선 및 건전성 확보 추진 
   2026 년 본사 추가 차입 계획은 없으며 해외법인은 기존 약정 차입금 일부 추가 인출 예정 
   2027 년은 기존 차입금 차환·운전자금 범위에서 최소한의 자금 조달 계획 
   영업현금 확대·투자 부담 완화에 따른 잉여 재원으로 차입금 감축 기조 지속. 구체적 조달 규모는 미제시

Q4-2. 유럽 IAA 정책 방향에 대응한 중장기 점유율 확보 전략은? 
  A. IAA 는 아직 최종안 미확정이나 수정 권고안상 외국인 직접투자·현지화 요건 강화 예상 
   배터리뿐 아니라 주요 소재의 현지 생산 역량 중요성이 확대될 전망 
   유럽 OEM 의 공급망 다변화로 현지 생산 체계를 갖춘 비중국 업체 소싱 니즈 확대, 경쟁 환경은 우호적 판단 
   폴란드 현지 양산 및 소재·부품 공급망을 이미 구축해 현지 배터리 소싱 수요에 신속 대응 가능 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 에 대한 유럽 고객 관심 증가. 폴란드 감가상각비 감소도 가격 경쟁력 확보에 기여 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 외 LFP·CTP 등 다양한 제품·솔루션으로 신규 수주를 확보해 유럽 점유율 확대 추진

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
As expected, the RBA Monetary Policy Board (MPB) lifted
the cash rate 25bps to 4.85% at its November meeting.
This follows the increase at the previous (late-September)
meeting and is a marked turnaround from the outlook back
in August. The post-meeting media statement cited higher

global energy prices resulting from the Middle East conflict,
tighter domestic capacity pressures and an increase in
inflation expectations for the decision.
However, the decision was a split vote, 6 votes to 3, a
possibility we have flagged for some time. In the post-meeting
media conference, Governor Bullock explained that the
dissenting minority put more weight on the downside risks and
did not see signs of wages growth picking up as much as the

staff forecasts implied. Those members were reportedly more
confident that slower growth would prevent higher inflation
expectations becoming entrenched.
Financial conditions are restrictive and this is expected to
bring inflation down over time. Because the RBA assesses the
risks to the inflation outlook to still be skewed to the upside,
the outlook for coming meetings will still be for a decision
between holding and hiking. But we note that the post-meeting

statement omitted September’s language “including increasing
the cash rate target further if needed” from the commitment to
do what is necessary to get inflation down.
In the post-meeting media conference, the Governor
commented that after five hikes, this is probably a good time
to “take stock”. Together with the dissents, we read this as
confirming our current view that the MPB will keep rates
on hold in December. There is also considerable data flow

between now and then, which could go either way, including
the Q3 national accounts.
Similarly, a lot could happen between now and February, but
we see it as the next most likely timing for a hike, should one
eventuate. We still see that as a risk rather than a base case.
Revised forecasts published in the accompanying Statement
on Monetary Policy (SMP) show underlying inflation lingering
around current rates for longer. This contrasts with the
previous SMP forecasts in August, which showed trimmed

mean inflation returning to the 2–3%yr target range by late
2027 and a little below the 2.5%yr target midpoint in 2028.
We note, however, that the conditioning assumptions on oil
prices, which are based on futures prices, were at a local peak
at the time the forecasts were finalised.
RBA hikes cash rate to new high of 4.85%, a December pause likely
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
The RBA’s GDP growth forecasts for 2027 and 2028 were
both revised down –0.3%pts to 1.3% for 2027 and 1.5% in

2028, despite an upward revision to the near term. This is
well below even the RBA’s downbeat view of potential output
growth. Within the GDP aggregate, recent misses and leading
indicators have required upward revisions to the near-term
outlooks for business and dwelling investment, and downside
revisions to public demand. These revisions were offset further
out with downward revisions to the 2028 outlook for business
and dwelling investment and an upward revision to public

demand. Household consumption forecasts were revised down
across most of the profile.
With the unemployment rate already at 4.7% in August and
September – a rate the RBA did not forecast until end-2027 in
its August SMP forecasts – the RBA has lifted its unemployment
forecast to show a peak of 5.1% in at end-2028, the end of the
forecast horizon. This largely reflects stronger labour supply,
with forecasts for employment growth essentially unchanged.

We have repeatedly flagged that cost-of-living pressures are
likely to keep people in the labour force and thus boost the
participation rate. The RBA had previously not factored this
adequately into its forecasts but acknowledged in the SMP
that this mechanism had kept participation higher than it had
expected at the time of the August SMP.
However, because inflation surprised to the upside recently,
this additional labour supply has had no bearing on the

RBA’s assessment of capacity pressures. The RBA assesses
the domestic economy to still be a little tight, and tighter
than previously judged. The RBA’s framework for assessing
the degree of spare capacity or tightness in the economy
necessarily translates an upside surprise on inflation (or unit
labour costs) into a conclusion that the economy must be
tighter than previously judged. Indeed, the RBA’s estimate
of the NAIRU (unemployment rate consistent with inflation

stable at target) has been revised up to 4.75%.
The RBA highlighted that elevated inflation expectations may
be contributing to higher realised inflation. Consumer inflation
expectations have started to lift again but remain below where
they peaked earlier this year. However, the RBA appears
“RBA lifts cash rate 25bps to 4.85% in split
vote, citing “broadly based” domestic inflation
pressures, Middle East conflict and AI boom."

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
to be putting more attention on the inflation expectations
of firms and the frequency of mentions of pricing in their
liaison program. This is putting a lot of weight on proprietary
information that is not clearly validated by other, longer-
running data sources.

The AI boom is heavily cited in the SMP and other post-
meeting communication. The RBA has lifted its forecasts
for major trading partners, especially high-income Asian
economies that are the centre of high-tech manufacturing.
Demand for AI-related goods is also cited as a driver of both
global and domestic inflation. And although the RBA has
revised up its near-term forecasts for business investment
on the back of strong data centre activity, it is working on

the assumption that Australia does not become ‘a significant
regional hub for data centres’. Nor does it allow for any boost
to productivity trends from the technology, even in 2028. This
is a very different view of the risks from AI than the RBNZ
expressed last week. For the RBA, it is all about inflation, a
little bit about pressure on domestic construction resources,
and nothing about either an ugly unwind of the current
financing boom or a future productivity dividend.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: whiplash
total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of
Australia’s data-centre investment boom, which should feature
prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3
National Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term

elections. In the House, Senate and a series of State Governor
races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come
2027, the Democrats will have a comfortable majority in both
houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-
of-living pressures. The election result also limits President
Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against
Iran. Notably, both immediately before and after the election,

President Trump and the administration have exhibited a greater
interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not
a surprise that Brent oil has backed off its most recent highs
around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to
near USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since
traded back up to above USD99, still 16% below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a
robust broad-based rally in major equity indexes, concerns

over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related
infrastructure buildout jolted US equities lower overnight – the
Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of
the prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and
FOMC rate expectations have also been scaled back; so, while
tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better
outcomes across the rest of the US economy may become
evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest

their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in
the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s concerns over US inflation
should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not
hike in December is a high bar, but the probability of further
hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate
by 25bps to 4.00% at their November meeting as expected.
On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to

rise from 3.5%yr at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity
data has also surprised to the upside in recent months, though
"there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other
prices and wages".
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase
at its November meeting, taking the cash rate to 4.85%. The
move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued
concern around inflation following the recent run higher in
global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation
is now not expected to return to the mid-point of the
target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth
expectations and the unemployment rate trending up to
5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with
three members preferring to remain on hold given downside
risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth.
Governor Bullock’s suggestion that it was time to “take stock”

after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference
to raising rates further “if needed”. In our view, February
represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but
we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible.
Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in October to now
be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline
is approaching the scale of previous housing corrections but

appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in
September, but this was driven by volatile unit approvals while
detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates
and the established-market downturn are beginning to weigh
on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile
rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3; however, much of
the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly

for fuel. Real spending volumes expanded by a much more
modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at
income, spending and saving flows. The latest update suggests
that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are
increasingly being squeezed, particularly those with low savings
buffers. With another rate increase now delivered, housing
wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like

the high-water mark for growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus
widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-monetary gold trade
yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The
most notable development was another exceptionally strong
result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the Q3
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
“The RBA and Bank of England both tightened

policy this week, in part due to price pressures
emanating from the Middle East."

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the
nascent improvement in energy price pressures over the
past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but
also conveyed that "Unless progress towards resolving the
conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk

to inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may
be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and comments made in
the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this
was a decision that needs to be made in December, but rather
a risk to be judged as additional information is received. This
points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027
than before, and only if conditions / risks warrant.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
No deals are done
This week’s developments underscore our message that
a rate hike at the RBNZ’s December meeting is far from a
done deal. New Zealand’s labour market remains softer
than expected, and that will be noted by those on the

policy committee who question the extent of second-round
inflation pressures. The rout in the US mid-term elections
has also taken significant heat out of oil prices for now,
although it’s unclear whether that situation will hold through
to next month. Our view on the endpoint for this OCR cycle
remains unchanged, but there’s a growing risk that the RBNZ
could slow the pace of tightening in the new year.
The September quarter labour market surveys pointed to

ongoing softness in the jobs market. The unemployment
rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing a four-year run
of increases, and reaching its highest level since 2015.
Employment was softer than expected, and wage growth
remains subdued despite increasing cost-of-living pressures.
The Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS) showed a surprise
0.1% fall in employment in the September quarter, after a
downwardly revised 0.3% increase in the June quarter. That

went against the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI) measure
of filled jobs, which had suggested some upward momentum
emerging in the jobs market (and notably, the HLFS measure
had outpaced the MEI for the previous nine straight quarters).
The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in
the labour force participation rate from 70.6% to 70.4%.
These co-movements essentially reversed the surprisingly
large increases that we saw in the June quarter, which we had

suggested at the time were more likely due to sampling error
in the survey rather than a genuine surge.
We generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate,
which is less vulnerable to sampling errors – and the message
here is one of softness. Our best interpretation is that, to the
extent that jobs are growing, it’s not fast enough to absorb the
growth in the population (which itself is a fairly low bar, as net
migration remains at a low ebb).

Even with the economy returning to growth over the last
year or so, hiring activity has remained weak. This may be
an ongoing legacy of employment trends in previous years.
Businesses have told us that they felt burned by the severe
skill shortages that developed over 2021-23, and looked to
hold on to workers to the extent they could during the 2024-
25 downturn. The flipside of this is that they may now find
themselves overstaffed even as activity picks up again.

This shows up most strikingly in labour force transition rates.
The rise in unemployment over the last few years has not
been driven by an increase in job losses, but by accumulation
of people who, once out of work, are finding it increasingly
difficult to get back in again. That also extends to young
people trying to enter the workforce for the first time – youth
unemployment of around 25% is the highest in the history of
the survey going back to 1986.

The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September
quarter, in line with our expectations overall but a bit weaker
in the details – private sector wages were up by 0.5%, while
there was a surprisingly large 0.7% increase in public sector
wages. There was a notable jump in the number of employers
citing cost-of-living adjustments as a reason for increasing pay
rates – although the size of those increases varied a great deal,
with many substantially less than the headline inflation rate.

Potential RBNZ implications
The labour market data was unequivocally weaker than
expected by either us or the RBNZ. While likely not a
gamechanger for the cycle overall, it will add ammunition to the
arguments of the doves on the Monetary Policy Committee that
a pause in interest rate rises will make sense soon – perhaps in
December or early next year. It’s likely the case that interest rate
markets were over-pricing the odds of a hike at the 9 December

MPS, so the subsequent pullback in pricing makes sense.
The message from the RBNZ in the October meeting was to
be careful about pricing in "done deals", and that the outlook
Michael Gordon
Senior Economist

### Page 7

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
7
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
will be data-dependent. As we noted at the time, alongside
measures of inflation expectations, the key domestic data
to watch ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December meeting will
be indicators of what is happening to excess capacity (the
output gap), as this will be a key determinant of the Bank’s

assessment of the outlook for inflation over the coming year.
The RBNZ looks at a broad suite of indicators of labour market
slack, although their research found that only four of them
seem to have predictive power: the unemployment rate,
the vacancy to unemployment ratio, the QSBO measure of
labour as the main limiting factor to growth, and the rate of
transition between jobs. The first three of these all pointed
to a softening in the September quarter. (The fourth one is

calculated from income tax data, so it’s not publicly available
and even the RBNZ receives it only with a lag.)
In the eyes of the dovish MPC members, the softness in
the labour market indicators will likely push in the direction
of having greater comfort with the medium-term inflation
outlook. The outcome of the election, with the possibility of a
return to a dual inflation/employment mandate, could increase
the prominence of these factors. This likely won’t be an explicit

factor in the RBNZ’s reasoning at its December review, but it
may be in the minds of individual MPC members.
That said, it’s important to remember that in the RBNZ’s
forecasting framework, the GDP figures are the main
determinant of excess capacity in the economy. The most
recent GDP data tells us that the output gap probably
narrowed a bit through the June quarter, mainly reflecting
historical revisions. In light of the latest labour market figures,

there may be some downside risk to the outlook for Q3 GDP –
but there are conflicting indicators there, so it’s not that clear.
At last month’s surprise on-hold OCR decision, Governor
Breman lamented that there was no protocol for the RBNZ
to be able to signal how it was reading the economic data –
in particular the softer than expected Q3 CPI print – ahead
of the policy meeting. In that light, it will be interesting to
see whether they use some already-scheduled speaking

engagements to signal what they make of the labour market
data. Top of mind is Chief Economist Conway's 10 November
speech to the UBS Australasia Conference (pointedly titled
“How is the NZ economy tracking”). Governor Breman is also
speaking to the Institute of Directors on 19 November.
Our view remains that the December OCR hike is more likely
than not. But there will be a clear debate to be had between
the MPC hawks and doves, and it may again come down to a

vote. We still expect a 4% peak in the OCR for this cycle, but
the time taken to get there could be more flexible. Takes along
the lines of "3% is the terminal rate" seem rather hasty on the
basis of a single data release.
Aside from how the Committee interprets the labour market
data, the key considerations in December will be: the RBNZ’s
surveys of inflation expectations by households and businesses;
whether the US mid-term elections lead to a sustained de-

escalation in the Middle East and a pullback in oil prices; and
whether developments in the AI space exacerbate some MPC
members’ concerns about the global economic environment.
And then of course there’s the result of the General Election,
with voting closing on Saturday evening. The actual formation
of a government is likely to take weeks of negotiation, so the
outcome may not be known by the 9 December MPS but at least
the range of possibilities should be clearer. We’ll publish our

initial thoughts on the election results early on Monday morning.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: Oct Retail Card Spending (%mth)
Nov 12, Last: –0.4, Westpac f/c: 1.4
We’re forecasting a 1.4% rise in retail spending in October.
While that would be a solid increase, it’s in large part due to
the 11% rise in petrol prices over the past month. Stripping out

fuel spending, we’re forecasting a 0.6% rise in core spending
categories. Core spending has been very choppy recently,
and the increase we’re assuming follows falls in the past few
months. A result in line with our expectations would leave the
trend in core spending relatively flat since the start of this year
with the rise in fuel prices weighing on spending in other areas.
AUS: Nov Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment (index)
Nov 10, Last: 84.4
Consumer sentiment fell 4.7% to 80.4 in Oct, returning to the

deeply pessimistic levels seen earlier in the year. Responses
over the course of the survey week showed a very sharp
deterioration after the RBA rate hike.
The Nov survey is in the field over the week ended Nov 7.
Interest rates will again factor with the RBA raising the cash
rate by another 25bps to 4.85%, an 18yr high. Average pump
prices also pushed higher, the national average just under
$2.40/litre, while the housing market downturn has continued

to broaden and deepen. The US mid-term election results may
have some effects on sentiment locally as well.
AUS: Q3 Housing Finance Approvals (%qtr)
Nov 11 Last: –5.2, Westpac f/c: –10
Market f/c: –8.5, Range: –15 to –5
The total value of housing finance approvals fell The value of
new dwelling finance approvals excl. refinance fell 5.2%qtr,
the largest quarterly drop in three years. Annual growth eased
to 7.3%yr. The weakness was accounted for mainly by lower

lending volumes: loan numbers were down 4.0%qtr (–0.6%yr),
while average loan size also contributed negatively.
Q3 is expected to see an even bigger decline with the full
impact of interest rate rises and tax policy changes showing
through. The value of both loan enquiries and turnover fell by
around 10% in the quarter. We expect total finance approvals
to show a 10% drop as well, with investor loan approvals likely
to be down 16%qtr and owner occupier approvals down 6%qtr.

### Page 9

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
9
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
US: Oct CPI (%mth)
Nov 11, Headline, Last: 0.6, Market f/c: 0.3, Westpac f/c: 0.4
US consumer prices rose by 0.6% in September, although
half of the gain was due to energy inflation, with core prices
instead up 0.3%. Over the year, prices were reported to have

risen 3.7%yr overall and 2.6%yr excluding food and energy.
Constructively, ex food, energy and shelter, annual inflation
met the FOMC’s medium-term target of 2.0%yr.
Come October, energy price pressures are set to recede
given the pre-election run higher in oil prices came late in the
month. A softer print for shelter is also likely to moderate core
momentum. We see a 0.4% gain for the headline measure and
a 0.2% result for core inflation as most probable. Looking to

2027, disinflation is expected to continue at a measured pace
towards the medium-term target.

Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
09:49 November 13 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest
reading since late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of severe economic stress.
Renewed cost-of-living and interest rate pressures were front and centre following another lift in

inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage
holders are feeling the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group falling to a record low.

### Page 2

Consumers have become noticeably more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the relevant
sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge
up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business survey showed confidence slipped further
to –15 in October as conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic. Of concern for the
outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow materially faster than selling prices, further
compressing already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter financial conditions can jolt activity. The
value of new dwelling finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly decline in the series’
history, taking approvals 25% lower year-to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined with the housing tax changes announced in May.

Owner-occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home buyers proving particularly sensitive
to the deterioration in affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum across the domestic economy,
consistent with Westpac’s expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the

consensus view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the result, but core inflation was
also marginally stronger than forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the past four
months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost
inflation through. Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace, gaining 0.3% in
October.
The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor

Waller prior to the release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and the labour market while
showing significant concern over the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the passthrough
of energy and technology input inflation. His conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the
monthly rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward supporting an increase in our policy
rate of 25 basis points at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI inflation data out

later today are similar to the past two monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent or
even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also kept the focus on inflation risks and
stated or implied the FOMC needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s updates,
pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by
September. We believe this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core inflation ex

shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-month annualised basis and the labour market is
balanced; but it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate, US households are under pressure from
the cost of living, and their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing costs. Highlighting

### Page 3

Related articles
this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and 2010. Our November Market Outlook will
provide a full update on our expectations for the US and the world when released on Westpac IQ this
afternoon.

Document 335: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3 according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever-volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2-0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues. As anticipated by our Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2). 
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%). According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2), driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export growth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports (-1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q). Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, growth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026, 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate (AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheless 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI-related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

► 美國經濟仍具韌性，惟通膨壓力猶存。觀察
美國9月ISM服務業指數表現，內需活動維持
穩健，企業投入成本壓力亦有所升溫；另一
方面，受地緣政治因素影響，美國柴油價格
大幅攀升，而柴油為卡車運輸、農業及製造
業的重要燃料，若價格持續維持高檔，恐進
一步增添通膨壓力。考量聯準會偏鷹立場，
且當前通膨降溫進程不如預期，年底前仍保
有升息空間。
► 美國債務總額突破40兆美元，預估2026年公
眾持有聯邦債務占GDP的比率將達101%，
財政可持續性風險升溫。與此同時，AI基礎

建設舉債熱潮，疊加中東局勢動盪帶來的通
膨不確定性，迫使投資人重新定價長天期債
券風險，要求更高的風險溢酬。隨實質殖利
率上行，美債10年期殖利率揚升並持續高懸
於5%水準之上，30年期長債殖利率亦同步站
上近20年來高點。隨長天期債券利率續揚，
殖利率曲線趨陡峭，債券配置宜先避開長天
期部位，聚焦中短天期債券鎖利機會。
熱門議題

### Page 6

投資焦點
► 距離美國期中選舉投票僅剩不到1個月，觀察
Polymarket賭盤預估數據顯示，眾議院選情
較為明朗，民主黨有超過9成的機率控制眾議
院。但參議院選情則尚有變數，今年以來兩
黨均互有領先，雖然數據顯示民主黨掌控參
議院機率呈現上揚，然隨時序進入選前最後
衝刺期，參議院能否亦由民主黨掌控仍待觀
察。故就選前而言，由於不同政黨的政策傾
向差異，潛在受惠產業亦有不同，選舉結果
的不確定性恐令投資人不易大舉押注股市。
► 另一方面，油價高檔徘迴，導致年底前通膨
擔憂仍存，加上AI企業發債需求成長、美國
財政體質惡化等因素共同發酵，推動近期美
債殖利率逐步攀高，並令股市預期本益比壓
力延續，進而限縮了投資人短期對美股的追
價動能，短期指數恐維持震盪輪動格局。可
留意抗波動、體質佳且具價值優勢之標的，
待期中選舉結束與3Q26財報展開後，不確定
性減退將有助資金回歸長線AI題材。
殖利率攀升令美股本益比面臨壓力參議院期中選舉選情仍具變數
期中選前擾動與殖利率走高，美股短期恐仍震盪輪動

投資焦點日股
► 9月日本銀行決議升息1碼，明確表示潛在
CPI已接近2%，未來也將繼續調高利率，政
策重點開始轉向防範通膨向上偏離目標。然
因有2位委員反對升息，加上總裁植田和男會
後談話偏向中性，令市場解讀此次升息未如
預期鷹派，美日利差不易快速縮窄，故9月會
議後日元反而呈現利多出盡的回貶反應。
► 觀察今年以來日股面臨短線日圓升值的狀態
並未出現顯著走弱態勢。一方面反映企業治
理改革推動股東報酬提升已成為支撐股價的
重要結構性因素。另一方面，升息代表日本
經濟已具備一定的健康程度，貨幣政策正常
化將不致對需求帶來衝擊。因此，隨BoJ延
續升息方向，將擴大銀行淨利差，改善其獲
利結構，並提升金融資產收益。儘管近期整
體日股預期獲利成長走勢略顯停滯，然日本
銀行股在總 體經 濟 與貨幣 政策環 境的 支持
下，獲利成長有望逆勢攀升，料將成為後續
資金關注重點。
日圓升值對日股影響似乎有限，關注銀行股獲利走強BoJ升息方向延續，惟其步伐仍取決於經濟與物價
BoJ政策保持正常化路線，日本銀行股有望持續受惠

### Page 8

8
股市
 公債殖利率走高雖可能壓抑股債市場近期動能，然而觀察美股第三季獲利年增率有望仍為雙位數成
長，或支撐股市表現。市場持續關注AI資本支出是否帶來獲利成長，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場
動盪，可分散配置於不同國家與產業，降低市場波動影響。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評
價面壓力等紛擾帶來干擾，可布局防禦股增強抗震力。長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且
基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融及醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。殖利率偏高環境建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走
升時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸

引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期抗波動，逢回可分批布局仍具長線趨勢之AI與科技產業

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 533: 今夜，英伟达财报焦点：没有黄仁勋，谁还能讲好AI故事？

Source PDF: all report pdf/今夜，英伟达财报焦点：没有黄仁勋，谁还能讲好AI故事？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

英伟达三季报预计营收 1090 亿美元，盈利同⽐增⻓约 90% ，但⻩仁勋突然离职令业绩本⾝
退居次席。汇丰将其评级从买⼊下调⾄持有，⽬标价腰斩⾄ 170 美元；摩根⼠丹利则担忧公
司可能暂缓发布明年业绩指引。市场更关注永久 CEO ⼈选、 FY2028 营收增⻓ 70% 的⽬标
能否兑现，以及 Rubin 量产、 AI 客⼾融资和出⼝管制⻛险。此次财报将成为检验英伟达能否
摆脱对⻩仁勋个⼈依赖的关键时刻。
摘要：
英伟达将于当地时间周⼆（北京时间周三凌晨）发布季度财报，但这⼀次，数字本⾝已退居次
席。⻩仁勋的突然离职，将这场原本例⾏的业绩发布会推向了华尔街数⼗年来最受瞩⽬的时刻
—— 市场真正想知道的，是没有他的英伟达，还能否延续那个关于 AI 的宏⼤叙事。
市场预计，英伟达第三财季营收将达到 1090 亿美元，盈利将增⻓ 90%。
⻩仁勋于 11 ⽉ 12 ⽇辞去英伟达总裁、⾸席执⾏官及董事会成员职务，起因是公司董事会正就婚
外情展开调查，此后⼜有两起与英伟达员⼯的婚外情报道相继曝光。公司随即任命⾸席财务官

Colette Kress 出任临时 CEO ，但永久继任者⾄今悬⽽未决。
消息发酵以来，英伟达股价已较 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇累计下跌约 22% ，过去⼀个⽉跌幅约 25% ，估值跌⾄
15 年低点，以未来⼀年市盈率计仅约 11 倍。
在此背景下，汇丰将英伟达评级从 " 买⼊ " 下调⾄ " 持有 " ，⽬标价从 365 美元⼤幅削减⾄ 170 美元；
摩根⼠丹利则维持 " 增持 " 评级，⽬标价 267 美元，但坦⾔不确定公司是否会在本次财报中给出明
年业绩指引。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Morningstar 分析师 Brian Colello 直⾔，" 如果英伟达出现业绩不及预期或指引疲软，我们会感到
震惊 " ，但他同时指出，本季度的基本⾯ " 在某种程度上是次要的 " 。
业绩本⾝料将强劲，但指引存在变数
从纯财务⻆度看，英伟达本季度的基本⾯并⽆悬念。
汇丰预计英伟达第三财季（截⾄ 2026 年 10 ⽉）营收为 1130 亿美元，⾼于管理层指引中值 1080 亿
美元以及市场预期，⾮ GAAP 每股收益预计为 2.55 美元，较市场⼀致预期⾼出约 3% 。
摩根⼠丹利亦表⽰，其渠道调研显⽰业务依然强劲，即便东南亚部分项⽬有所放缓，供给端仍
⽆法满⾜需求。
然⽽，市场更关注的是公司能否重申此前在⻩仁勋主导下发布的中⻓期⽬标 —— 包括 FY2028 财
年约 70% 的营收增⻓指引，以及客⼾需求接近 100% 同⽐增⻓的预测。
摩根⼠丹利指出，其⽬前对 FY2028 的营收增速预测为 52% ，低于公司指引的 70% ，认为该数字
已充分反映了下⾏⻛险，但鉴于临时 CEO 上任及其他不确定因素，公司是否会在本次财报中给

出明年指引，尚不明朗。D.A. Davidson 分析师 Gil Luria 表⽰，管理层在回应与⻩仁勋相关问题
时，很可能保持审慎克制。
继任问题主导市场情绪，估值重置尚未结束
⻩仁勋的离去，对英伟达的意义远超⼀般 CEO 更迭。
过去三⼗年，他将英伟达从加州⼀家餐厅⾥的创业构想，打造成市值逾 4 万亿美元的 AI 基础设施
核⼼企业，并以其对技术趋势的前瞻判断和扁平化管理⻛格，塑造了公司独特的竞争优势 ——
据汇丰报告，⻩仁勋曾直接管理逾 60 名直接下属，⼤量决策经由其本⼈拍板。
Colette Kress 接任临时 CEO 后，⻓期继任计划仍不明朗，英伟达董事会已启动内外部候选⼈搜
寻程序。
汇丰认为，在永久继任者确定并展现出执⾏现有产品路线图的能⼒之前，股价重估空间有限。
该⾏将⽬标市盈率从 25 倍⼤幅下调⾄ 11 倍（与当前估值低⾕持平），并明确表⽰， CEO 正式交接
及相关组织架构调整，预计在 2028 年之前不会对公司产品开发或竞争地位产⽣实质性影响。
Siebert Financial ⾸席投资官 Mark Malek 表⽰，"' 临时 ' 意味着真正的决定尚未到来，调查仍在进

⾏，周⼆的财报电话会议因此成为公司历史上最重要的⼀次。 "
三⼤核⼼议题：需求、供给与出⼝管制
除继任问题外，分析师和投资者将重点关注三个⽅⾯。
AI 需求与融资前景：摩根⼠丹利指出，中期选举后，⺠主党在多个反数据中⼼平台上取得胜
利，⼟地、电⼒和基础设施瓶颈的担忧明显上升。软银（英伟达 PORTS-Pike 项⽬合作⽅）已就
电⼒设备计提 37 亿美元减值。此外， CoreWeave 、 Nebius 、 IREN 等新兴云计算公司合计指引
2027 年资本开⽀约 1000 亿美元，但过去⼀个⽉其合计市值已缩⽔约 380 亿美元，融资环境的收
紧对英伟达⻓尾客⼾群体构成潜在⻛险。
Rubin 平台量产进展：本季度是英伟达⾸个⼤规模出货 Rubin 架构产品的季度，此前预期约占数
据中⼼营收的 20% （约 200 亿美元）。摩根⼠丹利指出，关键测试合作伙伴 KYEC 已连续两个季度

### Page 3

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

下调业绩指引，部分原因正是 Rubin 爬坡慢于预期，但分析师认为这不会对英伟达本季度营收确
认产⽣显著影响。
出⼝管制⻛险：汇丰对此进⾏了敏感性分析。根据过去六个季度的地区营收分布，美国占⽐约
69% ，其他国家和地区约 31% 。若对其他地区实施全⾯出⼝禁令（即损失约 11% 营收），汇丰测
算 FY2028 每股收益将较基准情景下降约 13% ，熊市情景下每股收益为 13.57 美元，较当前市场
⼀致预期低约 14% 。
电话会议本⾝谨慎措辞，难有 " ⽕花 "
多位分析师预计，本次电话会议将是英伟达历史上最受关注的⼀次，但实际信息量可能令⼈失
望。
StoneX 分析师 Cody Acree 表⽰，" 关注度显然会更⾼，但我不认为会有更多实质内容可说，他

们不会就⻩仁勋辞职的具体细节作出回应。"Interactive Brokers ⾸席策略师 Steve Sosnick 则警
告， " 有⽆数令⼈不舒服的问题可能抛向英伟达，错误的回答会受到惩罚。 "
值得注意的是，⻩仁勋此前并不在财报电话会议上发表开场陈述，但会积极参与问答环节，并
以其标志性的对 AI 变⾰性影响的宏观叙述著称。摩根⼠丹利指出，在新的管理层架构下，这种⼴
泛的 AI 热情表达在边际上可能有所收敛。
Gabelli Funds 投资组合经理 Hendi Susanto 则持相对乐观⽴场，认为英伟达已不仅仅依赖于⼀位
⾼管，" 他所创造的快速创新⽂化已融⼊英伟达的⼯程师和产品路线图之中，⾄少在未来两年
内，我们看不到对英伟达路线图的重⼤影响。 "

十一月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 金融 ‧ 台灣

中信金 
(2891.TW/2891 TT)

前 10月調整後獲利年增121%，提升 2026F配息能力 
重要訊息   
中信金前 10 月每股盈餘年增 1.8%至 3.45 元，但若加計 FVOCI 股票資本利得
之調整後每股盈餘則年增 121%至 7.57 元。 
評論及分析 
前 10 月調整後獲利年增幅度擴大至 121%。中信金 10 月稅後淨利月減
43%至 53.1 億元，帶動前 10 月稅後淨利年增 1.8%至 686 億元，達凱基原
先全年預估 82%。但若加計銀行、壽險之 FVOCI 股票資本利得 803 億元，
中信金前 10 月調整後淨利為 1,489 億元，年增 121%，已達凱基先前全年
預估 118%。其中前 10 月中信銀獲利年增 18%(淨利息收入及手續費收入各
年增約 20%，抵銷呆帳年增 20 億元)，抵銷台壽保稅後淨利年減 27%。 
2026F 可分配盈餘超過 2,000 億元。管理層先前表示 2026F 現金股利配發
仍需考量明年各業務成長規劃及壽險上繳獲利需視主管機關規定。由於
3Q26 中信金每股淨值季增 3.3%至 35.55 元，凱基推估其他權益仍維持超
過 700 億元水準。若年底仍其他權益維持正數，則先前提列特別盈餘公積
315 億元將迴轉至 2026F 可分配盈餘，預估 2026F 可分配盈餘將超過 2,000
億元水準。凱基下修 2026F 稅後淨利 1.3%，但上修調整後獲利 30%，反
映前 10 月調整後獲利優於預期，並上修 2026F 中信金每股現金股利 3-3.25
元(約調整後每股盈餘之 36-40%)，高於目前市場平均 2.8 元，預計現金股
利配發來源由銀行、壽險及其他子公司獲利上繳、金控銀行借款，配置比例
分別為 70%、7%、8%及 15%。 
預估 2027F 銀行核心獲利成長。由於美國重啟升息帶動美台利差擴大，加
上中信銀北美、OBU 及日本之外幣放款將受惠台灣供應鏈移轉，將有助提升
中信銀 2027F 外幣放款維持高個數成長，凱基預估 2027F 中信銀調整後淨
利差有機會維持 2026 之 1.7-1.73%水準，2027F 淨利息收入維持中個位數
成長。此外，管理層表示若台灣央行每升息 12.5 基點，將提升中信銀淨利
差約 0.9 基點。此外，由於中信銀前 10 月財管手收年增率達 30%，凱基預
估 2027F 財管手收至少維持中個位數成長，反映 2027F 台灣經常帳順差擴
大帶動超額儲蓄成長至 11 兆元，資金活水有助維持銀行財管動能。 
投資建議 
凱基預估 2027F 中信銀外幣放款成長將持續受惠台灣供應鏈移轉，帶動
2027F 淨利息收入年增維持至少中個位數，手續費收入維持中個位數成長，
加上 2026F 可分配盈餘充沛提升中信金每股現金股利上看 3-3.25 元，現金
股利殖利率 4%-4.3%，投資建議維持「增加持股」，目標價 84 元，基於總
和評量法，約當 2027F 股價淨值比 1.9 倍。 
投資風險 
全球金融市場轉弱；銀行信用成本大幅上揚。 
增加持股‧維持

圖 20: 公司概況  圖 21: 組織結構圖 
中國信託金控成立於 2002 年 5 月，為以銀行為主體之金控公
司，2022年 9 月資產達 2,522 億美元，台灣 16家金控中排名第
3。 中信金旗下涵蓋的金融業務範圍橫跨銀行、證券、保險、投
信、保險經紀、創投與彩劵業務等，中信銀同時亦是國內銀行中
最國際化金融機構，目前中信銀除在台灣擁有 147 家分行，中信
銀海外子行包括美國、加拿大、印尼、菲律賓及 2014 年 6 月購
併之日本東京之星銀行，4Q21 取得泰國 LHFG 集團增持股至
46.6%，成為 LHFG 集團最大股東，海外分行包括香港、新加
坡、大陸、印度、泰國及越南。中信銀為台灣消金業務龍頭，在
財富管理業務及信用卡業務均取得領先地位，在衍生性金融商品
交易亦居首位

台灣 中信金

十一月 12, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 8

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足
能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質
能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 
(PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3) 
公司員工人數與業務量於 2015 年開
始大幅增加，使 2015 年之後的溫室
氣體總排放量比過去明顯增加。未
來三年減碳目標訂定 2021 年至
2023 年減少 3.5%碳排總量。 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 
(PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總
量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
2020 年廢棄物處理支出金額約為新
臺幣 420 萬元。未來中信金以 2020
年為基準年，訂定 2021 年至 2023
年減少 3.5%廢棄物總量。 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
營運用水無取用任何地下水，各辦
公大樓及營業據點用水僅供員工及
顧客使用，對水源未有負面影響。
未來三年節水目標訂定 2021-2023
年用水總量減少 3.5%。 
環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處
理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗
員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
目前女性主管人數占所有主管人數
56.29%，其中中階主管及高階主管
的女性比例亦較 2019 年成長了
2~3%。 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_3653 健策_向子慧_20261111.pdf
